=== पृ. 10 (ocr) ===
9. --
१ बंतमानवकाळ (PRESENT TENSE)

A vert that refers to the present time is said to be in the present
tense, e, g., नोकर घरी जातो (A servant goes home); मुठ्या व्यायाम करतो
(A boy takes exercise).

Now read the following sentences ( arate वाक्ये वाचा.) !--

मी सकाळी दृध पितो. माझे वडील चहा Gara. माझी आई कॉफी झिते.
माझी बहोण कोको पिते. माझा भाऊ दूध fai. माझे अनेक मित्र चहा

तू सकाळी काय fata? मी सकाळी दृध पितो. पितात.

आम्ही सकाळी चहा तुम्ही सकाळी काय

Ret ar?

(1) There are three gendeis in Marathi : Masculine (qfsa), Femi-
nine (aft) and Neuter (नपुसकळिंग ). There are also three persons
as in English; the first person (94% पुरुष), the second persona (fetta -
पुरुष) and the third person (तृतीय पुरुष)

(2) The words in the bald type in the sentences above are the
present tense forms of the verb (क्रियापद) पिणे (to drink) :

(3) Every verb (Prati) has a root (धातू); the root of the verb
is usually arrived at by dropping the final ‘ णे! in the verb., e. ४., खांणे
(to eat) is a verb, ‘qj’ is the root. Sumilacly घांबणे is the verb, धाव
is the root,

We now classify the verbs in the above sentences according to the
persons. genders and numbers they govern.

The root अस (to be) also yields optional forms, e.g., असतो, असतात,
etc, The root हो (to become) yields होतो etc. ;

Unlike English, Marathi follws the S. O, V. (Subject, Object, Verb )
pattern; that is, in a Marathi sentence, the subject with its enlarge-
ments comes first and the verb comes at the end of a sentence. The
other parts of speech generally come in between the subject and the
verb, €. g., मुलगा आंबा खातो (The boy eats a mango); हुब्रार मुलगा फार
अभ्यास करतो (A clever boy studies hard); अधीक्षक लिपिकावर फार रागावतात
(The superintendent gets very angry with the clerk),

3

=== पृ. 13 (ocr) ===
Verbs are of two typs : (1) Intrausitive (अकर्मक ) and (2) Traasitive
( सकमक ) The former do not govern a direct object while the latter
do. The verb धावतात in the sentence मुले मैदानात धावतात (Boys run on
the ground) is an example of the first type; the verb टाकतो ia the
sentence नोकर पत्र टाकतो (A servant posts a letier) is an example of the
second type. In Marathi, the articles ‘a’, ‘an’, ‘the’ are generally
not translated, as Marathi does not have articles. .
Some verbs and their roots are given below :---
sana (Intransitive ) सकर्षक ( Transitive)
१, जाणे (जा) to go १. खाणे (खा) to eat
२. येणे (ये) to come २. पिणे (चि) to drink
३. धावणे (धाव) to run दे. लिहिणे (लिहि) to write
४. पडणे (पड) to fall ¥. वाचणे (वाच) to read

A Noun is a word used as the name of a person, place or a thing,
“. ४., राम, मुंबई, पुस्तक. री

The words in the bold type in the passage above are nouns (नामे),
In Marathi, there are three kinds of nouns (and not five as in English ):
(1) Proper Noun (विज्ञेष नाम), (2) Common Noun (सामान्य नाम ),
(3) Abstract Noun ( भाववाचक नाम) (All Material Nouns and Collective
Nouns are classed as Common nouns), The nouns in the bold type in
the above passage are classified below :-

छ

(1) A Proper Noun is the name of a particular person or place ( moun-
- tain, river, sea, road, book, building, city, etc.), e, ४. , राम, हिमाल्य,
गोदावरी, अटलांटिक, जनप्रथ, गीतांजली, ताजमहल, नागपूर.

(2) A Common Noun is a name common to every one of a class of
"objects, ¢, &., नदी, पेटी, टोपी, देवाळ्य, रस्ता, फळा, मित्र, घर, दि वस.
(3) An Abstract Noun is the name of a property or of a quality either in
an individual or in an object, e. g., नम्रता (Modesty), शौर्य
(Bravery ), प्रामाणिकपणा (Honesty ), सोंद्य Beauty), समाधान
( Satisfaction ). It is easy to distinguish between proper and
common nouns, Abstract nouns (भाववाचक नामे) in Marathi
are derived in two ways—(1) by the addition of the Sanskrit,
terminations य, त्व, ता to some of the nouns and adjectives, e. g.,
क्रूर (cruel) + यज्ज्क्रोय (Cruelty); मित्र + त्व्च्मित्रत्व (Friendship),
कटु (bitter) + at—=agar (Bitterness); (2) by the addition of
Marathi terminations to nouns and adjectives पण, gar, इ, की, गिरी,
€. 8, देब न. पण = देवपण (Godliness); चांगला + पणा ऱ्स्चांगुळपणा
(Goodness); माळक + इेन्य्मालकी (Ownership); भापला + की ages
(Afinity); गुलाम (a slave) + गिरीच्च्गुलामगिरी (Slavery); सावध

(Cautious) + गिरी च्ऱ्साबधगिरी (Cautiousness ),

(1) Proper nouns are sometimes used as Common nouns, e. g., (2) तो
आधुनिक कर्ण आहे (कर्ण--॥812 of Pandava’s brother; a noble person),
(२) आमच्या वर्गांत एक कुंभकर्ण आहे ( कुंमकर्ण--18112 of Ravana’s brother;
a person who always sleeps),

2) Common nouns are sometimes used as proper nouns, e. g., जीवन
हृ gure aa आहे ( जीवन-- life; water; name of a person),

(3) Sometimes aa adjective is used as a noun, ९. g., (१) झांधळ्य़ाला
पैसा द्या (आंघळा-- णात, a blind person), (2) शआद्वाण्याला शब्दाचा मार
( शह्ाणा--%52, a wise man),

(4) Sometimes an indeclinable ( अव्यय ) is used as & noun, e. इ. त्याची
वाहवा (indeclinable) झाली (He was praised )

3 लिंगाषचार (GENDERS) i
In the last chapter, we studied different kinds of nouns. Every noun
has a gender (लिंग), a number (वचन) and a case (विभक्ती ), In this ;
chapter, it is proposed to consider genders in Marathi. It may be stated ;
that genders of nouns in Marathi are generally determined more by i
practice and usage than by specific rules. In the absence of precise |
rales, the best way to learn genders is by practice alone.
Notice, -e.g. the three nouns फळा (a black-board), खुर्ची (achaic)and ~
डेबळ (a table), All the three articles are made of wood and yet the t
three nouns have three different genders Masculine, Feminine and é
Neuter, respectively. Hence, it is proposed to give mere indications, by कि
way of guidance only. They may help the learner to recognize the t
genders of Marathi nouns. न ‘ ; i

=== पृ. 30 (ocr) ===
Some Sanskrit nouns have been taken over in Marathi, without, any
change either in their structure or in their gender, e. g., U7, यल,. क्रम,
द्वेष, (all masculine); भार्या, पत्नी, नदी (all feminine); धन, वन, शरीर, नगर,
नाटक, दैव (all neuter ) न

Besides the nouns referred to above, there are many other nouns
(ending in अ) in Marathi for which no rules of classification can be
laid down, Some of these are listed below :— : न

Masculine (पु.) Feminine (eft. ) Neuter (न.)

दोर, जोर, कागद, विस्तव, तक्रार, फिर्याद, चूक, सरकार, शहर, गाव
नळ,' नांगर, पाट, घाट, तार, कंबर, पाठ, अडचण, (1. & प.) काळीज,
घोट, RATS. मोटार, - मनगट, नाक, तोंड, ,
न : कपाट, दार, कुलूप,

generally used to indicate the génders of nouns, e.g., हा रुमाल, at रुमाल,

=== पृ. 46 (ocr) ===
las ९
६ विभक्तिविचार (१) CASES (1) 7
By ‘case’ is meant the inflected form of a noun. This form of a .
noun indicates its relation with the other words in a sentence, e.g.,
'राजाच्या मुलाने प्रधानाच्या सुलीशी लग्न केळे (The king’s son married the mini-.
ster’s daughter). In this sentence, the inflected form‘ राजाच्या) of the noun :
‘ust’ indicates its relation with the noun ‘ मुलाने!, the inflected form.
प्रधानाच्या | of the noun ‘sq’ indicates its relation with the noun —
“मुलीशी '; the inflected form ‘gett’ of the noun “मुलगा? indicates its . .
relation with the noun “मुलीशी '; and the inflected forms “ मुलाने' and
*मुलीशी ' indicate their relation with the verb लग्न केले, न
The inflections in bold type in the above sentence are calledcase —
endings ( प्रत्यय ) and there are eight principal relations expréssed by
these case endings, These eight principal relations are kn Own as कारक 5
in Marathi. Hence, there are eight cases (विभक्ती) in Marathi, (1)
Nominative (प्रथमा ), (2) Accusative or objective (द्वितीया), (3) Instru-”
mental (तृतीया), (4) Dative (चतुर्थी), (5) Ablative (पंचमी ), (6) Posse- :
sive or génetive (षष्टी), (7) Locative (सप्तमी ), (8) Vocative (संबोधन).

७ विभक्तिविचार (२) CASES (॥) न

In this chapter, we propose to discuss the different cases, their mean- न

ings and illustrations. .
प्रथमा विभक्ती (The Nominative case ).-- . .
-1, The nominative denotes the subject. The subject is generally in the
nominative case, -e.g., सचिव 'आदेश काढतात ( The Secretary issues orders ).
In this example the-noun सचिव, whichis the subject, is in the nomina-
* tive case. र ;
2, The complement also takes the nominative case as in अधीक्षक अवर
सचिव होतात. (The Superintendent becomes an Under Secretary).
* In this example अधीक्षक is in the nominative case and अवर सचिव also |
is in the same case because it is the complement. —~ -
, हरितीया विभक्ती (The Accusative or the Objective case ):-— -

1. The Accusative case is like the nominative uninflected and.ex- - न
Presses the direct object of the verb, ८.४., सुलगा .दूध पितो, गुरुजी मराठी
शिकवतात. In these sentences the nouns qa and मराठी are direct objects
and are the uninflected forms of the nouns: If the object is inanimate,
no Case endings are added. .

2.. When a person or a living being is the object, the noun takes :
on the case endings (स, ला in the singular and @, 81, ना in the plural)
(68. अधीक्षक लिपिकांना बोलावतात, दुर्जन सज्जनाला फसवतो. Here the nouns .-
_. लिपिकांना and’ सज्जनाला are in the. accusative case and the nouas have

aken or the case endings at and ला. न : :

3. In, the case of irrational animals, the case endings are optionally

used (1. €., sometimes used and'sometimes not) e.g., नोकर घोडा आणतो,
Up नोकर घोड्याला आणतो. ० :

ह तृतीया विभक्ती (The Instrumental case)—~ .
: The endings of this case are ने and शी in the singular and नी and at
4 in the plural. (1) This case denotes the instrument (साधन ). by which न
: the action is performed, e.g., लोक हातांनी काम करतात, Fea चाकूने पेन्सिळ
: तासतो. Here the nouns हातांनी and 'चाकूने are instruments.
य (2) The instrumental case denotes. the subject or the १४९. ० the .
verb in.the past tense and in the potential mood, e.g., ईश्‍वराने or देंवाने
4 पृथ्वी निर्माण केली, मुलांनी देगा केला, विद्यार्थ्यांनी अभ्यास करावा, In these sen-
1 tences the nouns ईश्वराने, देवाने, मुलांनी and विद्यार्थ्यांनी ९ the subjects ली
: the respective verbs. . a न
- (3) The instrumental. case .also dendtes the sense “with”, e.g.,

् function with their family); (२) मुळगी बाहुलीशी खेळते (The girl plays with
क a doll ). : - : न
| 4 न ( 4) It also denotes ‘cause’ and ‘ manner’. (१ ) उपासमारोमुळे त्याने

षष्ठी विभक्ती (‘The Genetive case).— .

The case endings are चा, ची, चे (in the singular) and 2, च्या, ची in the
plural, (1 ) The genetive case is used to express the relation of origin
or possession, existing between objects or persons, अधीक्षकांचा मुलगा पदवी
परीक्षेत उत्तीणे झाला (The Superintendent’s son passed the degree exa-
mination ).. Here the relation between अधीक्षक and मुलगा is expressed by
the case-ending ‘aT, ( 2) The genetive case is adjectival in character;
it qualifies the noun by which it is governed e.g., |

माजा मुलगा. माझे मुलगे, * .
माझी मुलगी, माझ्या मुली. .
- माझे घर. माझी at .

(3) It also expresses the material and contents e.g., जोंधळ्याची भाकरी, . |
गव्हाची Tal, खव्याची वडी, Tar भांडे, तेलाची बरणी, (4) It sometimes चा
expresses the object ९.४., आम्ही राज्यपालांचे आभार मानतो (We thank the
Governor ); बडील मुलाचे अभिनंदन करतात (The father congratulates the
son); तो माझा अपमान करतो (He insults me).
ससमी विभक्ती (The Locative case )-- :

"The Locative case is used to express the relation of place, destination,
location or position ¢.g., तू घरी बस, The post-positions आत and मध्ये are ;
used for the locative case e.g., सुंबद्वेमध्ये जोराची वृष्टी झाली (There were
heavy rains in Bombay). The case endings of the locative case are
a and §. The former is appended to the सामान्यरूप ण a noun ( e.8., घर-
घरात), while the latter is added to the nouns ending in भ; and that too
to the nominative form the noun and not the सामान्यरूप e.g., घर-घरी, 5
सकाळ-सकाळी, कान-कासी.
संबोधन (The Vocative case ),— .

The Vocative case is used in addressing persons e.¢., मुलांनो, अभ्यास
करा. लोकहो, माझे म्हणणे ऐका.

त
न ¢ सवेनामविचार (PRONOUNS)
A pronouns is a word that stands for or takes the place of a noun.
: It may refer to the noun mentioned before it or after it.
Now read the following passage and note the words in bold type :—
मो कार्यालयात Tet व शिपायाला विचारले, “ माझ्याकडे कोणी आले होते काय १”
तो म्हणाला, “ साहेब, आपण तर आताच आलात, अजून कोणी आले नाही; पण
काल जे WEY आले होते, तेच आज येणार आहेत. ही त्यांची चिड्डी ”.
मी म्हणालो, “ते जर CS १-३० च्या दरम्यान आले तर तु त्यांना आत सोडू
. नकोस ”". -
“ठीक ane”, असे तो म्हणाला,
. The words in bold type in the above passage are pronouns, e, g. मी
तू, तो, जे, माझ्याकडे, ही, कोण, etc.
There are six kinds of pronouns, viz. : (1) The Personal ( पुरुषवाचक ),
(2) The Demonstrative (दर्शक), (3),The’ Relative (संबंधी ), (4) The '
Interrogative ( प्रश्नार्थक), (5) The Indefinite (अनिश्चित सामान्य) and (6)
The Reflexive or the personal indefinite आत्मबाचचक or सामान्य FEIT TH »)
. All the pronouns with exception of the first and the second person
pronouns ( मी. and तू, respectively ) are regularly declined. The Mascu-
line pronouns हा (this) तो (that) are changed to हो and ती in the
feminine and to हे and ते in the neuter, All the pronouns have a gender,
a number and a case, they have no forms in the vocative case,
"We now take up the various kiads of pronouns in order.
(1) The Personal pronouns (पुरुषवाचक सर्वनामे).--1'1656 are used for
names of persons and represent three persons. The speaker is
. the first person (प्रथम पुरुष); the person spoken to is the second

=== पृ. 72 (ocr) ===
: person ( द्वितीय पुरुष) and the person spoken of is the third person
( तृतीय पुरुष ) The forms of the personal pronouns are as
follows :—
(Singular) (एकवचन) (Plura) (अनेकवचन )
Ist person Hi (M.F.N.) आम्ही (आपण ) (M.F.N.)
त) (We )
2nd person तू (M.F.N, ) तुम्ही (आपण) (M.F.N.)
(Thou, you) (You) न
3rd person तो,ती, ते (M.F.N.) ते (M.), त्या (F.), ती (N.)
(He, She, It) (They) (४. 5. र.) -

It will be seen that the first and the second person pronouns are used
in ail the genders. The third person pronouns are also used as Demon- ।
strative proncuns—

e.g., ते फूल (That flower), ते लोक (Those people). The inflected forms
of the personal pronouns मी, a तो, ती and @ are given below :--

; Use of the Personal pronouns : ( 1) The first person मी and आम्ही,--
(a 3 The first person sing. is used when the speaker speaks of himself,
e.g., मी पत्र लिहितो, मी जेवणार नाही. (७) The editors of newspapers habit-
ually use the first person plural, e,g., आम्हास असे समजते (We understand
thus ) (We are informed); आम्ही असे ऐकतो ( We hear thus ). (c) A person .
placed/stationed in a high position may use the first person p ural
when speaking of himself, e.g., आमचे असे मत आहे की (1am of opinion
that). (१) When we want to express displeasure at the conduct we
sometimes speak in the first person plural e.g., आम्ही नाही तुमच्याशी
बोलत (I won't speak with you ) .

to the thing (i) that is present, (ii) that is mentioned or (iii) that is just

(When father heard this), (3) Sometimes these pronouns in the
masculine and feminine forms are used independently and stand by
theniselves, In this case they refer to a person or to a human being
only, ८.४., हा काय करतो आहे! [ What is this (boy/man) doing ?] ही कोण
आहे? [Who is this (girl)?], (4) The demonstrative pronoun असा

. or कोणाचा. is used when there is an inquiry of choice, e.g,, या गोष्टींपैकी
तुला कोणती गोष्ट आवडली? (Which of these stories did you like?)
(३) काय is used in relation’ to things, ८,४., त्यांना काय पाहिजे (What
do they want?) काय sometimes stands for the relative pronoun जे, ९.४.,
तो काय म्हणेल ते आण (Get him whatever he asks for), It is also 08९085 .
a particle of interrogative, e,g,, आज चांदणे पडेळ काय? (Will there be
moon-light to-day ?)

The indefinite pronouns ( अनिश्चित सर्वनामे )-—The examples of these
pronouns.are : (.१) कोण (Some one), (२) कोणी. (any one), (३) काय
(any thing), (४) कोणता (any one), (५) कैक (many a one), (६) कित्येक
(several), (७) काही (some, anything), (८) सर्व, सगळा (all), (९) परस्पर

: (mutual), .

Examples: (१) & चौकशी कोणाकडेही सोपबा (Entrust this enquiry to
any one); (२) येथे कोणी आले होते काय; (Had any one come here ?);
(३) wer काय वाटेल ते सांगा (Tell me whatever you like); (४) मी कोणता
निवडावा ! (Which one should I select?); (५) मी केक पाहिळे आहेत (I have
seen many a people); (६) कित्येक ठार झाले (Several were killed), (७) _
मला काही खावयास द्या (Give me something to eat); (८) सर्व स्नान करतात
(al their bath), (९) त्यांचे परस्परांवर प्रेम आहे. (They love each
other), .

All other genetive. forms such as त्याच्या, तुमच्या, तिच्या can be .
substituted for माझ्या and similar sentences can be constructed.

From these examples we can arrive at some important conclusions. .

1. The moment a noun (in any gender) is inflected the preceding 7
pronoun in the genetive changes its final vowel to या ¢.g.,—

(१) माझा मुलंगा (1॥,)--माझ्या मुलाळा/मुलाने/मुलात,
शि (२) तुझी खुर्ची (7. )--तुझ्या खुर्चीला/खुचींपातून/खुर्चीत.
. (3) तिचे घर (,)--तिच्या घराला/घरापासून घरात,
2. The rule holds good for nouns in both the numbers ९.४. .
कि (१) माझ नोकर (५. )--माझ्या नोकरांना नोकरांनी/नोकरांचा.
(२) त्याच्या इमारती--त्याच्या इमारतींना/इमारतींपासून/इमारतींत,
(२) आपली गाणी--आपल्या गाण्यांनी/गाण्यांपासून/गाण्यांत.
3. However, before feminine noins in tre plural only, the preceding
_ pronoun in the genetive changes its final vowel to या e.g., =
माझी मुलगी--माझ्या get,”
. त्याची पिशवी-ऱत्याच्या पिशव्या, . न

च Cc न
९ वबिदोषणविचार (ADJECTIVES)
- . An adjective is a word uséd with a noun to add something to its
न meaning, é.g., लहान सुलगा, आळशी नोकर, It thus limits the signification
of the noun it qualifies,
Now note the adjectives, in the bold type, in the following
paragraph :—
औरंगाबाद येथे एक गरीब पण लहान कुटुंब होते. यशबंतराब हे या कुटुंबातील

प्रसुख Wee होते. त्यांची पत्नी प्रेमळ व कष्टाळू होती. सवतः ते अतिशय हुद्वार व

उद्योगी होते. त्यांना तीन मुळे होती. तिन्ही मुळे प्रामाणिक, आज्ञाधारक व अभ्यासू

होती. मोठा मुलगा विश्वविद्यालयात शिकत होता. दुसरा माध्यमिक विद्यालयात शिकत
होता. धाकटा प्राथमिक विद्यालयात शिकत होता. मोळ्या सुलाला शिष्यवृत्ती मिळाली

व तो उच्च शिक्षणाकरिता परदेशात गेला. कुटुंबातील सर्व माणसे निःस्वार्थी होती. असे

चांगळे कुटुंब फार क्वचितच दिसते.

[New words: शिष्यवृत्ती (४, )-- Scholarship; निःस्वार्थी (Adj, )—-Selfless;
afaa (Adverb )—- rarely, ]

The words in bold type in the above paragraph are adjectives,

(1) The words गरीब (poor), wert (small), हुशार (clever), उदार
(noble), भाजञाधारक (obedient), मोठा (eldest), धाकटा (youngest), are
words showing the quality of the nouns they qualify, e.g., गरीब कुटुंब

: (a poor family ), Hence, these are called adjectives of quality, (गुण-
विज्लेषणे ), . .

(2) The words एक, तीन, fret, दुसरा, सव are words showing quan-
tity, Hence, these are called adjectives of quantity ( संख्याविशेषणे )

(3) The words हे, असे are derived from pronouns; hence, they are
called adjectives derived from pronouns ( सार्वनामिक विशेषणे )

७९ .

Uses of adjectives $--

(1) When an adjective qualifies two or more nouns, it agrees with the
nearest, ८.४. मला चांगले विद्यार्थी व विद्यार्थिनी आवडतात (1 like good stu-
dents ) (2) When the subject denotes inanimate objects, the predicative
adjective, (7.९. the adjetive qualifying the predicate ) agrees with the last.,
&.8. त्याचा कोट व टोपी मोठी होती (His coat and cap were big), (3) Sometimes
two or more adjectives qualify a single noun,, e.g,, हे एक जुने आणि उंच
झाड आहे (This is an old and tall tree), (4.) The adjective may be used
as the object of a transitive verb, ७.४. आपण दुसर्‍याचे बाईट fag नये ( We
should never think ill of others ). (5) When there is a comparison
between two objects, the adjective is placed after the ablative case
(पंचमी ) of the noun with which another is compared, e.g. पाणी तेलळापेक्धा
जड असते, ( Water is heavier than cil ). (6) There are no suffixes in
Marathi for comparative and the superlative as there are in Sanskrit,
The sentence in the superlative degree could be as follows, e.g, ¢ i )
तो वर्गात'ल सर्व मुलांत gmc आहे (He is the cleverest boy in the class 3;
. र ९०

१० भूतकाळ-भाग १ --अकमॅक क्रियापदे (PAST TENSE
PART I—INTRANSITIVE VERBS)

The past tense is used to express the notion that an act has taken
place in time already spent or passed, e.g., वडिलांनी मलां गोष्ट सांगितली
(Father told me a story); बसंत मुंबईस गेला (Vasant went to Bombay),

Now, read the following paragraph and note the verbs in the bold
type :--

एकदम जोराचा पाऊस आळा, मी जवळच्या इमारतीकडे धावलो. माझ्याबरोबर
न माझे मित्र होते, तेही धावले. इमारतीच्या खाळी गर्दी होती, म्हणून आम्ही दुसऱ्या
इमारतीकडे धावलो. इतक्यात माझा एक मित्र आला, तोही पावसात भिजला,
थोड्या वेळाने पाऊस थांबला, मी घरी गेलो. माझे सर्व मित्र घरी गेले, माझा gear
शाळेतून आला, मुलगी शाळेतून परत आली.

The verbs in the bold type in the above paragraph are in the past
tense, ¢.g., आला is the past tense ० येणे; होते is the past tense of असणे;
AS is the past tense of जाणे, and so on,

The forms of verbs in the past tense are almost the same in the case
of transitive ( Baus ) and intransitive ( अकपॅक) verbs, In the case of
intransitive (aTan%-without the object) verbs, the verbs agree in number
and in gender with the subject, e.g., मुलगा घावला (The boy ran); घड्याळ
बंद es (The watch stopped); मुलगी घरी आली (The girl came home ).

_ In all these cases the verbs घावला, पडळे and आली agree with the
subject मुलगा, घड्याळ and सुलगी, respectively, in respect of number,
gender and case, , .
As against this, transitive verbs when used in the past tense agree
with the object; and the subject takes up the instrumental case ( तृतीया
: _ विभक्ती), ८.&., नोकराने रुमाल आणला, मुलीने भाजी आणली, लिपिकाने पत्र आणले.
The object in this case is uninflected (i.e., having no case ending )
RY ;

=== पृ. 104 (ocr) ===

ense, Therefore, it agrees with the respective objects रुमाल (M, ), भाजी
(F.) and 74 (N,), which are uninflected forms of the original nouns,

In this Part the forms of transitive verbs in the past tense will be न
dealt with. Now read the following sets of sentences 1-7
क कळ Tense Past Tense
१. लिपिक मसुदा (M.) लिहितो. १. लिपिकाने मसुदा (M.) लिहिला.
२. लिपिक मसुदे (M. ) लिहितो. २, लिपिकाने मसुदे (M.) लिहिले.
३, लिपिक ager (1॥.) लिहितात. ३. लिपिकांनी मसुदा (M.) लिहिला.
४. लिपिक मसुदे (M.) ठिहितात. ४. लिपिकांनी मसुदे. (1. ) लिहिले. |
In these sentences the object मसुदा is in the masculine. gender; in the
past tense the verbs लिहिला, etc., agree with the number, gender and |
the case of the object, viz., मसुदा, e.g., मसुदे लिहिळे, मसुदा लिहिला. The
subject लिपिक is changed to the instrumental case लिपिकाने ० लिपिकांनी
according to number in the original sentence.

and भाज्या respectively. The subjects मुलगी, मुली are changed to their

टाकळे and टाकली in the past tense agree with the object पत्र and पत्रे

We give below some additional transitive verbs :— :

(१) मुक्त करणे (to discharge, (२) कृपा करणे (to favour )
to relieve ),
(३) तक्रार करणे (to complain), (४) ata देणे (१० trouble ),
(५) तयार करणे (to prepare ), (६) दूर करणे (to redress ),
(७) निलंबित ठेवणे (to suspend ) (८) आग्रह धरणे (to insist on )
(8) प्रतिज्ञा करणे (to pledge ) (१०) निश्चय करणे (to resolve)
(११) मेळ mei (to reconcile), (१२) नजरेस आणणे (to bring to
न notice ),

We can, with the help of these data, arrive at some generalizations. In
the case of a transitive verb used in the past tense : (1) the verb agrees
with the object in gender and in number; (2) the subject is changed to
its instrumental case, ( case endings ने and नी ) singular number or
plural number, as the case may be, a

. This generalization holds good when the object is un-inflected .
(अप्रत्ययी कर्म ). But if the object is used in its inflected form, ie., when
it is used with a case-ending ( सप्रत्ययी कर्म ) the verb in the past tense is
used in the third person singular and in the neuter gender, itrespective of *
the number (singular or plural) of the subject and of the object, e.g.:—

Present Past

=== पृ. 114 (ocr) ===
१९ & भविष्यकाळ (FUTURE TENSE)

The future tense is used to express the sense of futurity, It is used
of an act that has still to take place, e.g., उद्या पाऊस पडेल (It will rain
tomorrow); मी उद्या आपल्याला Hea (I shall see you tomorrow); लोक
प्रार्थना करतील (People will pray)

Now read the following paragraph :—- - :

गुरुजी म्हणाले, “ RT वेळाने आकाशात ढग जमतील, काळोख होईल, जोराच
पाऊंस पडेल, आपण सर्व वर्गातच बसू. तुम्ही बाहेर कसे जाल? मी तुम्हाला गोष्टी
सांगेन; नंतर तुम्ही प्रत्येकजण एकेक गोष्ट सांगाल काय १” राम म्हणाला, “गुरुजो, मी
एक गोष्ट सांगेन, माझे मित्र पण.एक एक गोष्ट सांगतील'', “ ठीक आहे”, गुरुजी म्हणाले,

(९ 1) We use the future tense wherever we employ the words
‘should’ and ‘would’ in English eg., (१) त्याला कोण gts? (Who
would laugh at him?), (2) तृ जाशील अशी आमची खात्री होती (We were

- certain that you would go); तू जाणार अशी आमची खात्री होती (We were
certain that you will go),

(2) It is used in the first clause of the conditional sentence शह
आंघळ्याला मदत कराल किंवा पैसे द्याल तर फार बरे होईल (It would be better if
you would help the blind)

( 3) We sometimes use the future tense where we use the present
tense in English ८,४., तुला आवडेल/ वाटेल तसे कर (Do as you like or as
you please ), री ी

(4) The future tense is used to express the possibility as is expressed
by the auxiliary verb ‘may’ ८.४,, मळा कोणीतरी मेटेल/दिसेल (Someone
may meet me ),

१३ & काळबदल ( TRANSFORMATION OF TENSES)

In this chapter, it is proposed to illustrate the transformation of verbs
from one tense to another. All the rules in this respect have already
been dealt with in previous chapters.

The following two rules deserve a special mention :--

(1) In the case of transi*ive verbs, the verb in the past tense agrees

with the object and the subject is changed to its instrumental case. (2 }

In the case of the future tense, attention is invited to the conjugation

_ of transitive and intransitive verbs and also to the second person plural

of some roots ending in aq, इ, उ, ए, भो (जा, पि, धु, घे, हो, etc).

१४ क्रियाविशेषण अव्यये (ADVERBS) _

An adverb is a word which modifies the meaning of a verb, an
adjective or another adverb e, g., (१) घोडा जळद धावतो. (र ) हा आंबा
फार गोड आहे, (३) तो अगदी स्पष्टपणे वाचतो, In sentence No, 1, the adverb
जलद modifies the verb धावतो; in sentence No, 2, the adverb फार
modifies the adjective गोड; in sentence No, 3, the adjective अगदी
modifies the adverb स्पष्टपणे (९169119)

Now read the following paragraph :——

आज येथे काम नाही, उद्या इकडे या, तोपर्यंत आसपास लक्ष द्या. नेहमी चोहोकडे
पाहा, उगीच वेळ दबडू नका. तुम्हांस खचित काम मिळेल. प्रतिदिन काहीतरी काम
करा.. आजकाल परिस्थिती फार चमत्कारिक आहे. क्षणोक्षणी काय होईल ते सांगता

१५७ दाब्दयोगी अव्यये (POST-POSITIONS)
A post-position is a word कणाला, shows the realation of a noun or
a pronoun to some other word in the sentence, ८.४., तो खुर्चीवर बसला
(He sat in the chair); काही लोक जेवगानंतर सुपारी खातात (Some people
eat a betelnut after meals); आपल्या भावाबरोबर खेळावयास ज्ञा (Goto
play with your brother),
Now read the following passage :-—.
महात्माजींचो प्रतिमा टेबलावर आहे. तिच्याकडे पाहा. महात्म!जींच्यामुळे आपण
स्वतंत्र झालो. त्यांच्या शिकवणोप्रमाणे आज आपल्पाळा वागले पाहिजे. त्यांच्याकडून |
आंपण पुष्कळ गोष्टो fast त्याच्यासारखा लोकम्रिय नेता sma झाला नाही.
गांधीजींच्या बरोबर काम करताना आमंद बुटत असे, त्यांच्याखेरोज दुसरा कोणीही

The words in the bold type above are post-positions,
Post-positions are of various kinds, depending on the relations they
signify ; :

(1) Denoting time (कालदर्शक)-आत (in, before); पुढे, नंतर
(after); आधी, अगोदर, पूर्वी (before); पर्यंत (until), ८.४., संध्याकाळपर्यंत
घरी ये. (Come home till evening),

(2) Denoting place ( स्थलदर्शक )- आत (in); मध्ये (in between);
माहेर (out); पाहणी, समीप, जवळ (near); समक्ष, पुढे, समोर .( before, in the
presence ); मागे (behind), खाली (under); वर (on); भडीकडे (on this
side); पलीकडे (beyond), e,g., माझ्याजबळ कोणी येऊ नका (Let no one
come near me ),

=== पृ. 128 (ocr) ===
(3) Denoting motion to or from a place ( गतिदर्शक ) — आतून
(from within); खालून (from below), मधून (from the middle); बरून
(from above ); पर्यंत (प); कडे (at, towards); पासून (from ), ८. g.,
पुलावरून जा (Go over the bridge ).

(4) Denoting iustrumentality (करणवाचक ) - कडून, द्वारा, हातून, हाती
(by or through), ८. &., त्याच्या हाती निरोप पाठवा (Send a message
through him )

(5) Denoting ability (योग्यतादर्शंक) -सम, समान, सारखा (like);
जोगा, जोगता (suitable); ८. ४., ageat गांधीसारखा पुढारी होणार नाही
(There will be no leader like Mahatma Gandhi ) P

, (6) Denoting contradiction (विरोधवाचक) -faea, उलटे (against ),

e. g., नियमाविरुद्ध कोणतीही TE करू नका (Do not do anything against

(7) Denoting co-existence (साहचा पैवाचक ) - बरोबर, सह, सकट, संगे,

(8) Pure post-positions (शुद्ध शब्दयोगी अव्यये )- च, मात्र, देखील;
पण, फक्त, ८, &४., फक्त दोन रुपये द्या (Give. only two rupees ),

(9) Miscellaneos (संकीर्ण) -साठी. (for); विषयी (about); भोवती
(around); पुरता (sufficient ); खेरीज, शिवाय, वाचून (without, except);
पेकी (out of),

Sufficient illustrations of the post-positions have been given in the
Chapter on cases.

(1) The post-position serves the same purpose as the case ending.
The noun to whicn it is joined assumes its सामान्यरूप (oblique form )
before the post-position, ८. g., पर्वतावर, इमारतोजवळ, घरापासून.

(2) Sometimes the noun, in question, may not assume the सामान्यरूप,

न Words denoting time come under this category, e.g., दुपारपर्वंत, सकाळपर्यंत.

( 3) Nouns and pronouns to which post-positions are joined either
take the सामान्यरूप 0 the सामान्यरूप s of its genetive form, e. ४, रामाजबळ
or रामाच्याजवळ, त्याकरिता, ०! त्याच्याकरिता, त्यापासून 01 त्याच्यापासून,

१६ Sarees अब्यथे (CONJUNCTIONS)

A conjunction is a word which j>ins together sentences and some-
times words, e.g., राम वनवासात गेला व भरतं अयोध्येत आला (Ram went to
the forest and Bharat came to Ayodhya), They generally make
sentences more compact, ८.४., श्री, हजारे ब al, aTIg उत्तम खेळाडू आहेत
(Shri Hazare ane Shri Naidu are the best players), This sentence is a
short way of saying : श्री. हजरे उत्तम खेळाडू आहेत ब श्रौ. नायडू उत्तम खेळाडू
आहेत. Sometimes the conjunction joins words only, ८४., राम व
लक्ष्मण हे माऊ होते (Rama and Laxman were brothers )

The conjunctions are classified thus —

(1) Copulative Conjunctions (समुच्चय बोधक), - These unite sente-

~ nces in form as well as in meaning, Examples: आंगि, आणखी, +

(and); ही, Tats, पण (also); म्हणजे (That 15), सफस्चंद व डाळिंब ही

फळे होत (An apple and a pomegranate are fruits )

(2) Disjuactive ( विकल्प बोधक ).—- These join sentences together
but disconnect their meaning, Examples : वां, किंबा, अथवा, अगर (or).
तू आलास भथवा तुझा भाऊ आला तरी BTSs (It will do if you or your
brother co nes )

(3) Adversative (न्यूनाध्रिक्प बोधक ), -- These coa ect sentenses, but
express the coutrariety of senses, Examples : पग, परंतु, किंतु (but);
यद्यपि, जरो (Though); तरी, तथापि (still), तानाजी पडला पण. सूर्याजी
घाबरला नाही ( Tanaji fell but Suryaji was not afraid )

(4) Causative or effective ( कारणदर्शक and परिणामदर्शक )~ These
state a reason or a purpose, Examples : कारण, कारण की (because,
in order that); म्हणून (therefore); यास्तब, याकरिता (therefore); आता
(now, in the present circumstances), at खूप अभ्यास केला म्हणून तो
परीक्षेत उत्तीर्ण झाळा He was successful at the examination because he
studied hard)

RRR

=== पृ. 132 (ocr) ===
वी (5) Conditioned (संकेतदर्शक).-- This expresses a condition or a
supposition, Examples: जर (if) with the connective तर (then );
बरी-तरी (although-still); agtat (otherwise); म्हणजे (then); को
(then), जर चांगले काम करशीळ तर पदोन्नती मिळेळ (If you work well you
will get promotion ) :

(6) Showing features ( EIT ).- These express the features,
Examples : म्हणजे (means, that is to say); म्हणून (as), विद्यालय OH
विद्या मिळण्याचे ठिकाण (A school is a place of learning),

(7) Objective (उद्देशद्दक),--- These denote the object or the
intention. Examples : म्हणून, सबब (therefore ), मठा हे गणित सुटत नाही;
म्ह्णून मी तुझ्याकडे आलो. आहे (1 cannot solve this example, therefore
(that is why ) 1 have ccme to you),

१८ वतैमानकाळ व त्याचे प्रकार (THE PRESENT TENSE
AND ITS VARIETIES )

In this chapter it is proposed to deal with the varieties of the
present tense in Marathi, There are four ways in which the Present
Tense is used, (1) The simple present or the present indefinite (साधा
वर्तमानकाळ ) as expressed in (a) नोकर काम करतो, (6) वडील मुळावर रागावतात
(The father gets angry with the son), This use has been dealt with in
the first chapter, (2) The present continuous (अपण वर्तमानकाळ) as .
expressed in (a) मुलगा खेळत आहे (The 9: y is playing), (0) धोबी was ge

* आहे (The washerman is washing clothes), (3) The present perfect ( git
वर्तमानकाळ ) as expressed in (a) आम्ही पत्र लिहिले आहे (We have written a
letter), (0) नोकराने भाजी आणली आहे (The servant has brought vegetables),
(4) The habitual present (रीति ब्तमानकाळ) as expressed in (a) मासे
समुद्रात राहात असतात (The fish live in the sea), (6) amet रोज व्यायाम
करीत असतो (We take exercise every day),

We now take up these varieties in their order, |

न (7) Simple Present (साधा वर्तमानकाळ) : This has already been dealt
with in the first chapter,

(2) The Present Continuous (अपूर्ण वतमानकाळ) : This represents an
action as going on at the time of speaking; it thus denotes an
incomplete action, It is also known as present progressive, From the
two examples given above, viz, (१) मुलगा खेळत amg and (2) धोबी
कपडे qq आहे, we can casily distinguish this from the simple present.
The simple present contains a simple verb as in नोकर काम करतो, वडील
रागावतात; whereas in the present continuous you wiil find compound
verbs such as खेळत आहे, ga आहे. In such compound verbs, ८. ४.; खेळत
आहे the first verb is the principal verb (खेळणे ) and the second is an

=== पृ. 137 (ocr) ===
auxiliary verb (असणे), The compound verb is formed by dropping % of
the verb, e.g. खेळणे-- tae and then by adding त to the root, eg.,
खेळ + त = खेळत; and then by adding the appropriate forms of the verb
असणे (आहे, आहोत, आहेस ) etc, the auxiliary verb and not the principal
agrees with the subject.

We give below the complete forms of the ver बसगे.

बसणे (to sit)
Singular Plural
Ist person—H} बसत आहे (M, F.) आम्ही/आपगण आहोत (M. F.)
(1 am ‘sitting ) ( We are sitting)

2nd person—g बसत आहेस (M.F.) तुम्हो/अपग बसत आह'त (शि. ७.)
. (Thou are sitting)- (४०५ are sitting)

3rd 87500-तो/तो/ते बसत आहे ते/त्या/ती बसत आहेत

(श. उ. प.) (M. ६. र.)

: (He/She/It is sitting) (They are sitting)

It will be seen that the above forms are common to all genders.
Intransitive verbs घावगे, चालणे, रडणे, हसणे रागावणे, भांडणे yield similar
forms as those mentioned above, eg., मी धावत आहे. आम्ही चालत

' आहोत; तू रडत आहेस, ते भांडत आहेत.

Some verb such us करणे, ठेवणे, आणणे, पाठवणे, दाखवणे, ढकलणे, विसरणे,
ठरवणे, काढणे, जिंकणे, ऐकणे and mostly transitive verbs except बोलणे,
म्हणणे, सांगणे, चालणे, बघणे optionally take इ before त, ८, ४., आणत, आणित
करत, करीत; ढकलत, ढकलीत; etc. Transitive verbs ending in आ,ई, ऊ, ए
do. take त only, ८. ४. गात, पीत, वृत, घेत, देत.

(3) The Present Perfect Tense (पुर्ण वतमानकाळ) : The present perfect
denotes an action that has just been completed, ( g ) त्याने उदाहरण
सोडवले आहे. (He has solved the example) (२) तुम्ही आंबे खाल्ले आहेत .
(You have eaten mongoes), (2) मूल शाळेत गेले आहे (The child has
gone to school), (४) मी पत्रे लिहिली आहेत (I have written letters). (५)
तू घरी बसला आहेस (You have sat at home), (६) लोकांनी राष्ट्रपतींचे स्वागत
केले आहे. (People have welcomed the President.) -

The verbs in the bold type in the above sentences are in the present
perfect tense. .

From all these examples it will be seen that the present perfect is
formed by adding auxiliary verbs such as आहे, आहेत, etc, to the main ग
verb in the Past Tense.

१२९

=== पृ. 139 (ocr) ===

Past Tense, the next mentioned आहेत, आहेस are the auxiliary verbs and —
they are the forms of the verb असणे. All rules applicable for converting a
verb into the Past Tense are applicable here, e.g,, (1) Changing
' the subject to the instrumental case, (2) The transitive verb (with
some exceptions) agreeing with the object, (3) Intransitive verbs, and
some transitive verbs agreeing with the subject, : | :

(4) The Present Habitual Tense (रीति वर्तमानकाळ) : The Present
Habitual Tense expresses:the habitual condition or occupation of the .
agent, न न

(१) तो नेहमी खरे बोठत असतो (He always speaks the truth )

(2) दौर्घोद्योगामुळे यश मिळत असते (We get success owing to constant
pre-occupation /Perseverence brings success ),

(३) एक fact कपडे शिवत असतो आणि दुसरा मापे घेत असतो (One of the
taliors sews the clothes and the other takes measurements )

(४) शिक्षक aight सांगत असतात आणि आम्ही त्या ऐकत असतो (The teacher

tells stories and we listen to them )

The verbs in the bold type in the above sentences are in the habitual
present tense. Although their translation is similar to that of the verbs
in the present tense, still there is a differe..cc between the two, eg.:

मी चहा घेतो (1 drink tea) (Present Tense), मी चहा घेत असतो (1 am
in the habit of drinking tea or I am used to drink tea-I am accustomed
to drink tea or I usually drink tea), Thus in the case of घेत असतो;
the tense denotes the habitual condition or occupation of the agent, .

The verbs in the bold type in the above examples indicate that these
are also compound verbs. The principal verbs are the same as those

१३१ न

=== पृ. 141 (ocr) ===
in the present continuous and the auxiliary verb is असणे and its regular
forms in the present tense, e.g, जात असतो, येत असतात, भांडत असतोस,
करीत असतात, आणत असतो.
The forms of a verb (जाणे ) in the habitual present tense are given
below :~
- रीती वत॑मानकाळ (Habitual Present):
: जाणे (to go)

१९ भूतकाळ व त्याचे प्रकार
(THE PAST TENSE AND ITS KINDS)
The Past tense, like the present one, is of four kinds. न
( १) The simple past tense ८. ४. मुलगा शाळेत गेला (The boy went to

school); fat ge तोडली (She plucked flowers ), (2) Continuous past
tense (अपूर्ण भूतकाळ) ८. ४. मुलगा शाळेत जात होता (The boy was going to
school); ती He तोडत होती (She was plucking flowers), (२) Past perfect
tense (पूर्ण भूतकाळ) e. ४, मलगा शाळेत Her होता (The boy had gone to
school ); तिने ge asst होती (She had plucked flowers), (४) The
Habitual past tense (रीति भूतकाळ ) ८. ४. मुलगा शाळेत जात असे (The boy
used to go to school); ती फुले तोडत असे (She used to pluck flowers )
Out of the varieties referred to above the first one (The simple past
tense) has been previously dealt with, We take up the remaining in .
their respective order, - न

(1) The continuous past tense (अपुर्ण भूतकाळ ), The past continuous
represents an action as going on at some point in the past time, FT
मी त्याला पाहिले, तऱ्हा ता पत्ते खेळत होता (when I saw him he was playing
at cards). The method of arriving at the forms in this variety is the
same as that in the c: ntinuous present except for the auxiliary verb,
e, ४. धावणे, HW ,drop णे and add त, we get the roots qa, कर; add त to
the roots घावत, करत; now add the forms of she verb अखण in the past
tense, e, ४. होतो, होतास, etc, We thus get the forms जात होतो (was going),
ज्ञात हाता (were going), जात grat (were going). The past continuous
tense can be said to refer to a past action begun and in progress but: not
finished ८, ४. अधिकारा. चोकशी करत होत. (The officer was inquiring)

The forms of बाचणे in the past continuous tense are given below: —

(2) The Past perfect tense or the pluperfect tense ({F YARIS). ——
The past perfect denotes an action completed at some point in the

. past time, before another action was commenced 2.g., आम्ही बाहेर आलो
तेव्हा पाऊस थांबला- gat ( The rain had stopped when: we came out );

अधिकाऱ्यानी या विषयावर चर्चा केली होती (The Officer had discussed this
matter ).

The verbs in the above sentences are in the past perfect tense.
Such verbs are arrived at by joining the appropriate forms of असणे
in the past tense (८: 8. होता, होते, eat), Take any verb in the past
tense say तो Ret ( He went ) and use it with the forms of auxiliary
verb असणे in the past tense (होता ). Thus we get तो गेला होता (He had
gone). Take another sentence wrt चर्चा केला. (He discused) in the
past tense; use this with the necessary forms of the verb असणे in the
past tense ( होती ); we then get the sentence त्यान चर्चा केळी होती (He
had discussed ), All rules regarding the conversion of a verb in the
past tense discussed previously, hold good in this case also.

This tense is also used when an action takes place, at some specified
period in the past and does not contmue at the at present time, e.g.

१३१७

at काल पुण्याला गेलो होतो (I had gone to Poona yesterday )

that of the habitual present, only the auxiliary verb असणे is tobe used
in the past tense ८. ४. असे, असू, असत, etc, -

व ती असे (F.) त्या असत (.) .

(1 used to write), आम्ही गात असू (We used to sing)

The future tense has only three varieties : (1) The simple future

=== पृ. 160 (ocr) ===
The Habitual Present tense (रीवि वतैसानकाळ )-—The negative forms
of verbs in the habitual present are formed by adding @ to the roots
and by adding the forms of नसणे (variety 2) as required by the perso-

‘ pal pronouns or nouns e, ४. मी जात नसतो, मी जात नसते (Ido not
usualy go); मुलगा बाचत नसतो (The boy does not usually read or is
not in the habit. of reading); आम्ही भांडत नसतो (We do not usually
quarrel ),

The ordinary rendering of these Sentences in English would be ‘I do
not go’. ‘The boy does not read’ and ‘We do not quarrel’. The reader
may thus think that the ordinary present tense and the habitual present
tense are alike. It is only to distinguish the latter from the former that
we use the word ‘usually’, which distinguishes the habitual prescnt
tense from the ordinary present tense, :

We now give below the use of some verbs in the negative in ali the
varieties of the present tense :—

२४ प्रश्नाथेक वाक्ये (१) [Interrogative Sentences (1)]

Interrogative sentences are formed with the help of interrogative

"pronouns and interrogative adverbs, The interrogative pronouns are

. who (कोण), what (काय) and which (कोणता-ती-ते ), how many (किती),

The interrogative adverbs are when (केव्हा), where (कोठे), why (का) and

- how (कसा, कशी, कसे), All of these play an important part in asking
questions, We deal with these in their order,

- (१) ‘Who’ (कोण) is used of persons only, e.g., who came (कोण
आले १) who says so? (असे कोण म्हणते १) who is the officer here ! (येथे
अधिकारी कोण आहेत १ ). In all these questions we expect the answer to
be the name of a person. (2) When the agent is unknown, कोण (who)
is used with the verb, in the neuter gender and singular number, e.g.,
कोण बोलते १ (who speaks 2) कोण बोलावते ! (who calls?) कोण टंकलेखन
करते १ (who types?) (३) As an-indefinite pronoun कोण (who) means
* some one’, e,g., खाली कोणी आले आहे (some one has come downstairs ).
In this sense, there may not be a question, but in the sentence खाली कोणं
आले आहे 2 (who has come downstairs 2), there is a question and there

. is a stress on the word काण, The pronoun कोण is changed to कोणी in the
instrumental case both in the singular and in the plural, ८.४., चहा कोणी
केळा (Who prepared tea?) दूध कोणी आणले १ . (४० brought milk?)
चित्र कोणी काढळे 2 (Who drew the picture 2), The answers in Marathi,.
to all these questions will have the subjects in the instrumental case,
either in singular or in plural, as the case may be,

The interrogative form कोण ( who) has different forms in different
cages, Naturally, there will be so many questions in Marathi which

२५ प्रश्नाथक वाक्ये (२ ) [ Interrogative Sentences (1!)]

In this chapter it is proposed to deal with the interrogative adverbs
(१) When 2 (केव्हा १), (2) Where ? (कोठे), (३) Why? (arf), and
(४) How? (कसा, कशी, कसे १), When adverbs are used in asking
questions, they are called interrogative adverbs,

. (१) केव्हा (when),-— This is an adverb of time, ¢.g., (१) आपण केव्हा
आलात? (When did you come?) (2) तुम्ही स्मरणपत्र केव्हा पाठवले १ (When
did you send a reminder?) Also, शिवाजीचा जन्म केव्हा झाला १ (When was
Shivaji born ?)

The post-positions करिता and साठी when joined to केव्हा refer to the
period of time, हे ओषध केव्हाकरिता आहे! (At what time of the day/when
is the medicine to be taken?) ही भाजी केव्हासाठी आणली are? (For what
part of the day are the vegetables brought? )

The word %eagrqtga also denotes the period of time, 2.४., तो केव्हापासून
उभा आहे ? (How long has he been standing?) The words केव्हाचा;
केव्हाची, केव्हाचे ४7७ also used to denote the period of time and are
used in different genders according to the nouns they govern, e.g., तो
केंव्हाचा आला आहे, ती केव्हाची अभ्यास करत आहे. ते मूल केव्हांचे रडते आहे. The
words केव्हाचा, केव्हाची and केव्हाचे are to be translated as ‘Since a long न
time’, The sentences cited above are mere statements and not ques-_
tions. (These can be turned 110 questions, e.g., तू केव्हाचा अभ्वास करत
आहेस ? ( How long have you been studying? )

The interrogative adverb कधी is substituted जि केव्हा. It also refers to
time, The interrogative adverb कधी also yields forms like कधीकरिता,
कधीपासून, कधीचा, कधीची, कघीचे. . :

(२) कोठे (where).— This is an adverb of place, e.g., कार्यालय कोठे
आहे { (Where is the office?) आज अधिकारी कोठे जाणार आहेत? (Where is
the officer going today? )

The adjectives कोठला (M), काठळी (F), कोठले (N) and कोठचा (M),
कोठची (F), कोठचे (N) are formed from the interrogative adverb (कोठे)
and are used in different genders according to the nouns they govern——

हा कोटला/कोठचा मुळ्या आहे (What place is the boy from?)
ही कोव्ठी/कोठची मुल्गी आहे? (What place is the girl from?)

, ट्रे कोठले,कोठचे पत्र आहे? (What place is.the letter from?)
The adverb कोठपासून. igs refer to the time, ९. ४., तुमचा पगार कोठपाचून/ .
कोठून काढली [From what time (date) is your pay ताविण्शा?] The adverb
कोठपर्यंत refers to the limit, ९. 8., तुम्ही कोठपर्यंत जाणार? (How far do you
mean to go ?) :

(3) का (why).~ This is an adverb of rcason. It means ‘why’,
‘wherefore’, ‘for that reason’ and the answer implies the reason of
the action expressed in the verb, ९. g.,—

तुग्हास का उशीर झाला? (Why are you late?) The words. प्रयोजन or
कारण BA? are sometimes substituted for का, e. ४., तुम्हास safle होण्याचे

substituted for का, ८. ४., 2 कशाकरिता आलात? (Why have you come
here?)

The interrogative adverb का occurring in the body of the sentence
means ‘why’, but when used at the end of a sentence it turns the
sentences into a question. -

आपण येणार का 2 (Wili you come?) तुम्ही टिप्पणी लिहिळीत का? (Did you
write the note? }.

(४) कसा, कशी, कसे (iow).—In what manner? These are interrogative

२६ आज्ञाथे (The Imperative Mood )

So far we have used verbs in all the tenses, either to make a statement
or to ask a question, A verb may be used to express a command also,
e.g. कोपऱ्यात उभा राहा (Stand in the corner घरी जा (Go home), It
may be used to express a condition, e.g., पाऊस पडत AIS तर येऊ नका
(Do not come if it rains), It may be used to express a duty, ९.४., प्रत्येकाने
नियमाप्रमाणे वागावे (One should behave according to rules ), These diffet-
erent modes or manners in which a verb may be used to express an
action are called moods. There are three moods in Marathi :-

(1) The Imperative Mood (atari), (2) Thé Potential Mood
(विध्यर्थ) (3) The Conditional Mood ( संक्रेतार्थ), In this chapter we
propose to deal with the Imperative Mood,

The Imperative Mood can strictly be used in the second person, e.g. ,

_ (8) खाली बस (sing.) (sit down); खाळी बसा (plural); (sit down); (२)
पुस्तक दे (give book) (sing.); पुस्तक या (give book) (plural); (3) पाणी पी
(drink water) (sing,); पाणी ar (drink water) (plural), But in the first
and in the third persons the sense of the verb is expressed by the use
of the auxiliary verbs in English, viz., may, let, e,g., मी येऊ काय? (May
Icome?),; छोक सुखी असोत (May the people be happy); तो जावो (Let
him go),

We give below the forms of three different verbs : बसणे (to आ), घेणे

री (to take) and लिहिणे (to write), in the imperative mood :- |

P _ Uses of the forms of Imperative Mood. The personal forms of
verbs in the imperative mood have different meanings :-
(1). First person (प्रथम पुरुष ),-- The first person is used for making
an appeal or to ask for permission or to obtain consent, e.g.,
मी येऊ काय? (May I come?), I also expresses its model
sense, @.g., वेळा, सिनेमाला जाऊ (Come on, let us go to a picture),
(2) Second person (द्वितीय पुरुष). -11 the second person it expresses
a command or an advice, ९.४., चालता हो (Get away); कार्यालयात वेळेवर
at (Come to the office in time ), It also expresses a prayer or a request,
e.g., uftarer मदत करा (Help the poor); एरप्रत्तांना मदत aT (Give help
to the flood-affected people ), It sometimes expresses a persistent न
request ९.४., लोकहो, आभाळस सोडा (0 people, give up idlenss )
(3) Third person (तृतीय पुरुष ).--11 the third person, the impera- ;
_ tive is used to biess (आश्षीर्बांद देणे) or to curse (ara देणे ),-८.४., तो
छवकर परत येवो (May he soon return); तो aux वर्षे जगो (May he live
for hundred years); शत्रूचा पराभव होवो (May the enemy be defeated.
vanquished ), -
The negative imperative-form is made up by adding नको (singelar )
and नका (plural). These are joined to the infinitive of a verb,
The infinitive of a verb is formed by adding G& to the verb, e,g,,
, WEF—HNS, MH — Fe, I+ S— ag, येन-ऊ — येऊ, ९.४., येऊ नको
(Do not come—Sing.); येऊ नका (Do not come—Plural), Sometimes

नकोस, In the first person (Plural) only ' नको ' is used,
In the third person, the negative form is arrived at by adding, न,

२७ विध्यथे (The Potential Mood)

The potential mood ( विध्यर्थ ) sometimes called the subjunctive mood,
expresses duty, ९.४., त्याने जावे (He should go), कर्मचाऱ्यांनी वेळेवर यावे
(The staff should come ia time), नोकराने भाजी आणावी (The servant
should bring vegetables ), In all these examples the verbs जावे, यावे and
आणावी are in the petential mood and express the sense of duty,

(1) The potential mood expresses the sense of obligation, e.g., am
क्षमा मागावी (He should beg pardon ) (2) It sometimes denotes fitness,
ability or authority, eg., विद्वानांनीच चर्चा करावी (The learned alone
should discuss). (3) It expresses, surprise e.g., या प्रसंगी आम्ही काय
बोलावे? (What should we speak on this occasion?), (4) Sometimes it
expresses the sense of ‘must’, ९,४., मग त्याने काय उपाशी राहावे! (Must he
then starve?), (5) It expresses a wish, e.g,, देश संकटातून पार पडावा (The
country should tide over the calamity ), (6) It denotes the sense of
the phrase ‘is to’, e.g., आता यावरून काय बोंघ घ्यावा! (What lesson,
now are we to take from this १). (7) It expresses a possibility, e.g,, लबकर
पाऊस पडावा (It might, soon, rain), (8) As already stated it expresses a

’ sense of duty and obligation, The potential mood expresses this sense,
e.g, आपण मिकाऱ्यास मदत करावी (We should help the 0००), त्याने उद्या

. ३० प्रयोग (The Voice) न
The use of voice in Marathi is quite distinct from that in English,
whereas in English the voice is determined by whether the action is
performed by or upon the subject. In Marathi the voice is determined
by, whether the verb agrees in number and gender with the subject or
_ the object. e.g. the sentence मी आंबा खाल्ला is in the Passive voice,
(कर्मणि प्रयोग) though the sentence ‘I ate a mango’ in English is in the
Active voice,
How a single sent:nce can be transformed into passive voice, in all
the varieties of the threz tenses, the present, the past and the future, is

=== पृ. 200 (ocr) ===
, Although, an attempt has been made above to transfer a single
sentence from the active voice into the passive voice, in all the varieties
of tenses, it has to be said without any reservation that voice in the

न Marathi Language owes. great deal. to the Sanskrit language, The
Sanskrit language recognises three voices :

(1) waft (Active), (2) कर्मणी (Passive), (3) भावे (Imperson a construc- _
tion), The Marathi Language follows this classification. The classifica~
tion is based on the agreement or on the disagreement of the verb with
the nouns to which it is related, The agreement or disagreement is .
indicated by the inflection which the verb assumes in conjugation.
The noun with which the verh agrees may be cither its subject or its
object, Sometimes it may agree neither with the subject nor with the
object. but may be neutral, i. e. ‘indifferent to both. Hence the three
types of voices mentioned above.

(1) कतंरी प्रयोग (The Active or the subjective construction) —In_ this
construction the verb agrees with the subject in gender and in number
of the latter, e.g, रामा घरी जातो (Rama goes home); तू आंबा खातोस
(You eat a mango),

(2) कमंणी (The passive or the objective construction\—In this con-
struction the verb agrees with the object, ¢.g., (1) नोकराने भाजी आणली

"(The servant brought vegetables), (2) तिने काम केल (She did the
work), (3) त्यांनी गोष्टी सांगितल्या (They told stories), (4) विद्यार्थ्यांनी अभ्यास
करावा (Pupils should study). In the four Marathi sentences cited
above, the verbs agree with the objects, The English rendering of
these sentences would be strictly thus + Q) Vegetables were brought
by the servants, (2) Work was done by her, (3) Stories were told by
them, and (4) though this is not good English study should be done
by the- pupils, respeciively,

Ss far as the Marethi Language is concerned the definition of the
passive voice given above holds good, The verbs agree with the gender

१९१

=== पृ. 201 (ocr) ===
and the number of the object. Some sentences in Marathi such as
मला आंबा पाहिजे (1 want a mango ), (2) मळा आंबे पाहिजेत (I want mango-
es), (3) Fer ती भाषा येते (I know that language), (4) मला भाषा येतात (1
know languages), (5) त्याला ते उदाहरण आवडते (He tikes that example), (6)
wel उदाद्दग्णे आबडतात (He likes examples) display this principle. Hence
these Marathi sentences cited above are said to be in the passive con-
struction since the verbs agree with the objects in Marathi although this
is not the case in their renderings in the English Language.

(3) ‘at&@ (/mpersonal construction).—In this construction the verb
agrees neither with the subject nor with the object, but is always in the
third person, singular number, neuter gender. It is on this account that
this consruction is possible in respect of transitive or some intransitive
verbs, e.g. सचिवांनी अधीक्ष्कास बोलावले (The Secretary sent for the Super-
intendent); दुर्जनांनी सज्जनाठा फसविले (The wicked deceived the good); त्याने
पत्नीस बोलावळे (He called the wife); त्याला घरी जाववते (He can /is able to go
home). ‘In all these sentences the verbs do not agree either with the
subject or with the object, but are in the third person, singular num-
ber and neuter gender, irrespective ef the gender, number and the
person of the subject or of the object.

३१ घातुसाधित विदोषणे (The Participles )

A participle is that form of a verb which partakes of the nature -
“poth of a verb and of an adjective. We may also say that it is partly
a verb and partly an adjective, e.g, ay बसत आहे (He is sitting ); तो बसत
gat (He was sitting); तो बसलेला. ae (He would be sitting). The
forms in the bold type in the above sentences are participles formed
from the root बस (to sit ) .

Participles are known as ards in Marathi, Just as in English, we
have in Marathi the present participle (वर्तमान कृदन्त ), the past
participle (ya Gara) and the future participle (मविष्य कृदन्त ), There is
one more participle in Marathi called the pluperfect participle, We
illustrate these in their order. ©

, : ( 1) THE PRESENT PARTICIPLES (वतमान कृदन्ते )
Consider the following sentences :—
(१) मुलगा अभ्यास करीत आहे (The boy is studying )
(2) तो जाताना म्हणाला (He said while going ) :
(३) मुलगी खेळत असताना पडली (The girl fell while playing).’ .

. (४) नोकर काम करत असताना गात होता (The servant was singing

while working ). .
. (५) मी घावता घावता पडलो (1 fell while running), -
* (६) नोकराने न कळवित्ता नोकरी सोडली (The servant left the. job
. without intimating ).

The verbal: forms in the bold type. in the above sentences are
examples of the present participle. In English, all these participles end
in ‘ing’ but in Marathi they are formed by the addition of ( १)त (२)
ता (३) ता, ताना to the roots (HT, बस, खेळ, रड, इस, ९८. ). The suffix to ~
be used depends on the meaning to be conveyed.

(1) त. —Add this suffix to the root and you get the participle ending
ind, ८.४. जा +a = जात, बस -- T= बसत, खेळ + त = खेळत, All these are
generally translated into English by adding ‘ing’ to the verb, e.g. जात
। going); बसत (sitting ); खेळत ( playing). All transitive roots (except
the monosyllabic ones like खा, गा, पी, भी, धू, दे, घे), optionally take डी
before त, e.g, बाच + त र वाचत, but वाच -- ई-- त र वाचीत, Thus we get
करत--- करीत, फेकत--फेकीत, भाणत-- आणीत. The roots खेळ, पाहा, म्हण, शीक
and विसर however do not take §, They will therefore give the forms खेळत,
पाहात, म्हणत, शिकत, विसरत etc, Sometimes we come across such sentences
as मी धाबत धाबत घरी आलो (I came home running) where the present
participle aq is repeated. It denotes the continuance of action. This
present participale ending in त is used in the imperfect or continuous
tenses and in the habitual tense, examples of which are given below :-

(१) टिपिक ल्हित. आहे (The clerk is writing); (२) ठिपिक लिहीत होता
( The clerk was writing); (३) लिपिक लिहीत असेल (The clerk will be
writisg) are the examples in the present continuous past continuons र
and the future contihuous tenses, respectively, (२१) at गात असतो,
(२) तो गात असे and (३) तो गात असेल are the examples of the habitual
present, the habitual past and the habitual future teases.

(2) ता.- The suffix at is added to roots to form present participle,
e.g. खेळता, बुडता, वाहता. However, these are used as adjectives and
hence govern the nouns they follow, e.g. वःहता ओंढा (4 running brook).
(M.); वाहूती गंगा (A flowing river’) (F,); वाहते पाणी (Running water)
(N.). The Participles बाहता, वाहती, वाहते like other adject-ves endiny in
आ (चांगला, बरा, पांढरा ) chanpe their final vowels to या, the moment
the nouns: following them take up the case terminations or the post-
positions, ८.४. वाहत्या आढ्यात, वाहत्या ओढ्यापासून, TAT गंगेत, वाहत्या पाण्यात,
- (3) ता, atat,—These are added to the roots of the verb, e.g. जाता-

big crowd ), We can also form these participles with the help of the
auxiliary verb असणे (to be), e.g. खेळत--खेळत असता or खेळत असताना,
बाचत--वाचत असता or बाचत असताना, fedia--fedia असता or लिहीत
असताना. These can be translated as ‘while playing’, ‘ while reading’,
and ‘while writing’, respectively. They are adverbial phrases.

Besides these there are in Marathi, present participles ending णारा,
णारी, णारे. These suffixes are also added to the roots, ८.४. जाणारा (M.),

; जाणारी (F..), जाणारे (N, ), all meaning ‘going’; खेळणारा (M.), खेळणारी
(F.), खेळणारे (N.), all meaning playing, These participles are to be
treated like adjectives ending in आ. न

(2) PAST PARTICIPLES ( भूतकाळवाचक घातुसाधिते) ;

undergo all the changes which such adjectives generally undergo.

न There are two types of past participles also called ya कृदन्त. They
are active and passive, गेळेला (gone), मेळेला (dead) are the examples

” of the past active participles, while केलेला, लिहिलेला, STSSaT are examples

of the past passive participles. . .

(3 ) FUTURE AND PLUPERFECT PARTICIPLES
- . ; (भविष्यकालवाचक धातुसाधिते ) ,

Future participles express the intention or the desire of the person
to do an act expressed by the verb. They take the terminations णार —
and णारा which are added to the roots, e.g. जाणार, जाआारा, The parti-

जाणारे (All meaning going’). Although these are common in the pre- ~~

न sent ard tke future participles the reader can determine their meaning
from the context. : ;

यता आंटंबडा (the coming week); येत्या सामडारी (on coring Monday).

after having played ). Thesz are culled pluperfect participles and deno-

verb, e.g. (1) काम संपवून दुम्ही घरी जावे (You should go home after

mood. These are the dative and the genetive of the infinitives. The

(from कर), .

आवयास, आवबाला, but the suffixes करिता and साठी are joined to the
verbs and not to the roots, In. the process the fival © of the verb
changes to QT ८. g.—

लिहिणे -- साठी > लिहिण्यासाठी; लिहिणे + करिता = लिहिण्याकरिता,

३३ प्रयोजक क्रियापदे (The Causal Verb )
The causal verb is derived by suffixing aq to the root of the
Marathi verb,.e.g. कर (To 00 )--करव (cause it to be done); झोप
( sleep )-- झोपव (cause to sleep); ऊठ (get up)-3aq (wake up)
In the case of monosyllabic. roots (roots with one syllable = x, पी,
खा, जा, गा,--' aa’ is added to the roots. We get जा (80) - जावंबणे
(To cause or make to go); दे (give )-देवबणे (To cause or make to give )
All causal verbs are transitive and give forms like any other transitive
verbs, e.g., : .
मी बसवतो, आम्ही बसवतो (Present tense )
मी बसवले, आम्ही बसवले ( Past tense )
मी बसवीन, आम्हो बसवू. _ (Future tense )
त्यांना aaa; त्यांना बसवा (Imperative mood )
; तू बसवावेस, तुम्ही बसवावे ( Potential mood )

३४ ' हाब्दसिद्धी ( भाग १) [Word formation (Part |) ]

Every language has its own lexicon, Marathi is not ‘an exception to ;
this general statement. The words आंवा, खांदा, वाडा, देखावा, मेळावा are’
pure Marathi words, However, as days passed by words from other

. languages also crept into the Marathi language, e.g., the word स्टेशन

(from English); the word रजा (from Hindustani); the word तूप (ghee)

(from the South Indian language),

This however is not the object of this chapter. We-only want to show
how the Marathi lexicon has been enriched by the addition of words
from other languages. In this respect Marathi owes a great deal, to Sanskrit.

_ We will first show a number of Sanskrit words taken over into

Marathi, without any alterations. Such words are called तत्सम words,

(The list is not exhaustive.) ,

=== पृ. 221 (ocr) ===
The words cited above are originally Sanskrit words and have crept
into the Marathi language in their original from, i.e., without under-

; going any change in their contents, meaning and gender. Such words
are called तत्सम words. ;

There are also other words that have crept into Marathi, However,
these words have undergone a slight change in their structure and -
form, but not in their meaning and gender. Such words are called
तदूभव words (arising or emanating or derived from) e. g., (words from
Sanskrit are given first), .

Apart from the तत्सम and aqua words, a number of words are-
formed by the employment of prefixes (उपसर्ग) and suffixes (प्रत्यय) . The
prefixes are put before the nouns. We first deal with words formed
by the employment of prefixes. The prefix may have a meaning-of its

- own; but when it is joined to a word, there is a change in the meaning
of the original word, e.g., वाद (argument); भनुवाद (translation). There
are so many prefixes in English, e.g,, bed—a bed; in—income,
out —outcome.

न 1, Prefixes at and अनू.--भ is used before words beginning with a
consonant and अनू before words beginning with a vowel, e.g. Prefix

(incomplete), (3) स्थिर (steady)—arféuz (unsteady), (4) ज्ञान (knowledge)
-णअज्ञान (ignorance), (5) व्यवस्था (management) —a7eqqe1 (mismanage-
ment), (6) चल (movable) ary (immovable), :

. The Sanskrit language is full of these and compounds have added to

- Compound words are different from संधी, No doubt, both are com-
binations, but of different types, A compound is a combination of
words; a संघी is a combination either of two vowels or of two conso-
nants or of a विसर्ग and a vowel or a consonant, Some examples of संधी
have been given below :-- .

=== पृ. 236 (ocr) ===
३७ काही इंग्रजी क्रियापदे (Some English verbs )

There are in English many verbs which when translated or rendered
into Marathi affect the construction of Marathi sentences. English is
by no means deficient in such verbs.

Read the following English sentences :— .

(1) The sages subsist on roots and fruits.

(2) The Mogal Emperor was fond of architecture.
(3) Bhima was more than a match for the giant.
(4) We are afraid of serpents.

(5) People like mangoes. -

(6) He has three brothers.

In most of these sentences, you will find that verbs, adjectives and nouns
are followed by particular prepesiticns. Now note the renderings of
these very sentences into Marathi. :

(१) क्रषी कंदमुळांवर व फळांवर राहातात.
(२) मोगल बादशहाला शिल्पकाम आवडत होते,
(३). भीम राक्षसाला पुरून उरला. ,
(४) आम्ही सापाला मितो. न
(५) लोकांना भांबें आवडतात.
(६) त्याला तीन भाऊ आहेत.
You will thus find that the use of corresponding verbs in Marathi
sentences affect the construction of the sentences. Take. e,g., sentence
No, 5, “People like mangoes” (लोकांना आंबे भावडतात). The original
Subject ‘People’ in the English “sentence is changed to. लोकांना (Dat,
. Plu, of लोक); and the verb ‘like’ in the English sentence which agrees
with the subject ‘people’ in the English sentence agress with the object री
'आंबे' in the Marathi sentence, .
. न २२७

=== पृ. 237 (ocr) ===
Consider the last sentence, He has three brothers’ (त्याला तीन भाऊ:
आहेत). The subject ‘He’ in the English sentence is changed to त्याला
in Marathi and the verb ‘has’ which agrees with the subject in the
English sentences agrees with माऊ (brother) in the Marathi sentence.

र्‍ We will, therefore, deal with such verbs in this chapter and illustrate
their use in Marathi. The list of verbs given here is not exhaustive.

1. To want (qrfési),— (1) My brother wants money (माझ्या भावाळा
पेसे पाहिजेत). (2) She wanted a sari (तिला साडी पाहिजे होती), (3) The
child may want milk (मुलाला दूध पाहिजे असेल). (4) Pupils may be wanting
more marks (विद्यार्थ्यांना अधिक गुण पाहिजे असतील). न
. The verb 'पाहिजे' conveys. the sense ‘it is wanted’ €.g., मला ea रूमाल
पाहिजे can be translated as ‘I want a handkerchief’. The forms हवे, हवा,
हवेत, eal, etc,, of the verb होणे (to become) are ‘optionally used for
पाहिजे, ८.४., मढा रुमाल पाहिजे or हवा; मला रुमाल पाहिजेत or हवेत, मळा वही
हवी, मला वह्या हव्यात. .

The verb पाहिजे is joined to the past tense of the principal’ verb. It
then denotes the necessity of doing the action expressed by the princi-
pal verb, ¢.g., मला HS पाहिजे (1 must go), मळा धावले पाहिजे ([ must run),
तिला आले पाहिजे (She must come). न

If however the principal verb is transitive, the form of the past
tense of the principal verb agrees with the object e.g., तुला आंबा (M.)
खाल्ला पाहिजे; तुला भाजी (F.) खाल्ली पाहिजे; get केळे (N.) ars पाहिजे; तुम्हाला

, It may be observed that a person for whom or by whom something

The verb येणे is also substituted for शकणे, The verb येणे ordinarily
* means to come, but when it substitutes amar it has the same meaning
as that of the latter (to be able, can). It is joined to the present

- —_-write (आम्हांला लिहिता येते); He is able to walk (त्याला चालता येते);
The verb येणे may also be joined to the infinitive mood, e.g., ऐकू
येते (is possible to hear). There is a difference between नाता येते and
" ऐकू येते. The construction जाता येते has an active force while ऐकू येते
has a passive force. ऐकू येते means, ‘it is capable of being heard’ or
‘is audible’,
The forms येणे in the past and the future tenses when intransitively

The third person pronoun ‘it’ is many times used at the beginning
of English sentences, It is used as a provisional subject before the verb
_‘to be’ when the real subject follows, e.g.—— .
. It is easy to criticise (टीका करणे सोपे आहे) ,
It is certain that you are wrong (तुझी चूक आहे, हे निश्चित आहे) (तृ
चुकला आहेस हे निश्चित ). :
Tt is used to emphasise the noun or the pronoun following, ९.४,
(1) It was he who picked up the quarrel (त्यानेच भांडण उकरून
काढले).
(2) It was at Delhi that the agreement was signed (fastest करारा-
वर सह्या झाल्या ).
It is also used as ap indefinite nominative of an impersonal verb,
९.४.॥य न
117815 (पाऊस पडतो); It thunders ( गडगडाट होतो),
: . Jt is used in speaking of the weather or of the time, e.g,,——

=== पृ. 251 (ocr) ===
ve काही वाक्यरचना (Some Sentence-constructions)

In this Chapter it is proposed to illustrate certain Complex and
Compound sentences. Traditionally, a Simple sentence is one that has
only one finite verb e.g, (१) रामा सकाळी अभ्यास करतो (Rama studies in
the morning); (२) कार्यालयीन काम संपबल्यावर कर्मचारी घरी जातात (The staff
goes home after finishing the office work); (३) सेनिक प्राणपणाने लढले (The
soldiers fought to the bitter end). All these sentences contain only
One finite verb in each, viz., करतो, जातात, wees, Therefore, these are.
called Simple sentences (केबल वाक्य). :

A Compound sentence is one which is made up of two or more
co-ordinate clauses, e.g,, (१) चांदणी रात्र होती आणि आम्ही गप्पा मारल्या (It
was a moon-lit night and we chatted away); (२) रात्र झाली, खूप पाऊस
पडला आणि आम्ही सर्व भिजलो (Night came on, it rained heavily and we
were all wet), In these sentences there are co-ordinate clauses con-

रि nected by the conjunction ‘and’ such sentences are called’ Compound
sentences (संयुक्त वाक्‍य).

. A Complex sentence (मिश्र वाद्य) is one that contains one main
clause (मुख्य बाबय) and one or more subordinate clauses (गोण वाक्य), “.४.,
(१) जेव्हा संध्याकाळ झाली तेव्हा त्यानी विश्रांती घेतळी (They rested when it
was evening); (२) तो यशस्वो होणार हे निश्चित आहे. (That he will succeed

. is certain); (३) जो जरुरीच्या वेळेस तुम्हांहा मदत करतो तो खरा मित्र होय (A
friend in need is a friend in deed); (४) जे जे चकाकते ते सर्व सोने az (All
that glitters is not gold). In all these sentences there is one main
clause and one subordinate clause.

. It is not possible to exhaust the list of such sentences, We, therefore,
propose to give examples of some.—

1. जेव्हा--तेव्हा (when-then).—Thése two adverbs bring in a complex
sentence containing an adverb clause of time, ८.४., (१) जेव्हा पाऊस पडतो

superintendent gets angry); (3) जेव्हा मांजर दूर असते तेव्हा उंदीर खेळतात
(When the cat is away the mice play). The sequence of time and tense
has to be observed in the principal and subordinate clauses, Generally
there is the same tense in both the clauses; in some cases, however,.
the context may require one tense in the principal clause and another
in the subordinate clause. We give below examples of each type :—

8. कारण (because, अं1०९)--कारण (because, for, since) indicates
reason and denotes the closest causal connection, There are three
rendering in English of the word कारण : (1) Because—this denotes
the closest causal connection; (2) For—this denotes the weakest and
(3) since—this comes between the two examples, e.g. यंदा मिठाई फार
महाग आहे कारण साखर मिळत नाही (The confectionary is expensive at
present because sugar is not available); तो परीक्षेत उत्तीणे होईल कारण तो
हुषार आहे (He will pass in the examination for he is clever). Other
examples are given below :—

(१) आपण दूध पितो कारण त्यामुळे शक्ती येते.

(२) यंदा महाराष्ट्रात दुब्काळ पडणार नाही, कारण खूप पाऊस पडला आहे.

(३) रात्री झाडाखाली झोपू नय कारण झाडे कर्बे-द्वि-प्राणिळ वायू (Carbon-
| dioxide) सोडतात.

(४) aPaa रागावले कारण विधानसभा प्रश्नाचे उत्तर वेळेवर गेळे नाही.

(५) आम्ही मराठी शिकतो कारण ती राजभाषा आहे.

The word ‘since’ is also translated into Marathi by ज्याअर्थी and
expects the word त्याअर्थी (therefore) to complete the sense of the sen-
tence, e. g., ज्याअर्थी तू वेळेवर आला नाहीस त्याअर्थी तुला चहा मिळणार नाही.
(Since you did not come in time, you shall not get tea) .

5 Qvs

=== पृ. 257 (ocr) ===
9, इतका-की (so that).—Adverb clauses of result or conse-
quence are introduced by इतका-की (M.) or इतकी-की (F.) or इतके-की (N.)
e.g., (2) तो इतका चांगळा मनुष्य आहे की सर्व लोक त्याला मान देतात (He is so
good that he is respected by all), (२) हे झाड gah उंच आहे की मी
त्यावर चढू शकत नाही (The tree is so high that I cannot climb it), Some
times the adverb ‘too’ is substituted for ‘so. .that’, However, the
Marathi rendering of the sentences remains the same. Other examples
of this type are given below :—

(१) काम इतके आहे की एकटा मनुष्य ते करू शकणार नाही.
(२) तो इतका अननुभवी आहे की त्याला अधीक्षकाच्या पदावर नेमणे शक्‍य नाही.
(३) शहर इतके लांब आहे की कोणीही पायी जाऊ शकणार नाही.
र (४) साखर इतकी महाग झाली आहे की सामान्य मनुष्य घेणार नाही.
(५) नोकर इतका मंद आहे की वेळेवर कोणतेच काम होणार नाही.

10. म्हणजे (means, that is ).—This is a copulative conjunction, e.g.,
कार्यालय म्हणजे कामकाज करण्याचे ठिकाण [An office is (or means) a place
for transacting business ] . Other examples illustrating the use ० म्हणज्ञे
are as follows :——

(१) Breas म्हणजे दूध मिळण्याचे ठिकाण.
(२) मामा म्हणजे आईचा भाऊ. न
(३) चारात आठ मिळविले म्हणजे बारा होतात.
(४) एक रुपया म्हणजे शभर पैसे. ;
(५) गाडी थांबली म्हणजे आम्ही खाली उतंरतो.

४९ शाब्दसमसूहाऐबजी एक दाव्द
र (One word for a group of words)
' Marathi is rich in single or compound words which occasionally
require their sin:ple paraphrase or which when used by themselves add
to the beauty and brevity of the language, e.g., ही गोष्ट पुर्वी केव्हाही घडलेलो
नाहो (This incident has never happened before). The words in bold
type in the above sentence can be substituted by the word ‘ अभूतपूर्व
(unprecedented). The sentence thea will be ही गोष्ट अभूतपूव आहे.” The
former negative sentence is transformed into affirmative without any
न change in meaning.

Read the sentence ' महात्मा गांधी व.सरदार tees एकाच वेळी ह्यात होते
(Mahatma Gandhi and Sardar Patel were living at the same time), The
words in bold type in the above sentence can be substituted by the
word “समकालीन! (Contemporaries) and the sentence after the substitu-
tion of the word समकालीन will read as महात्मा गांधी ब सरदार पटेल सम-
कालीन होते. (Mahatma Gandhi and Sardar Patel were contemporaries).

The substitution of a single or a compound word for a group of
words does not always affect the grammatical construction of the
sentence. In some cases, however, the substitution affects the gramma-
tical constructicn of the original sentence. Read for example the
sentence त्या दोघा मित्रांचे aq सारखे आहे (The two friends are of the same
‘age). The word ‘anaqen’ (of the same age) can be substituted in
this sentence. The substitution of the single word समवयस्क, however,
affects the grammatical construction of the sentence. The sentence in
the new form will be 'ते दोघे मित्र समबयस्क आहेत '.

showing a substitution whcih does not affect the grammatical construc-
tion of the sentence and the latter showing the substitution which
affects the construction of the sentence.
